# Remaining core kinetic harness gaps

This is a filtered index of the freshly generated [kinetics report](kinetics.md). It preserves remaining work rather than treating new generator coverage as full closure. Counts are missed branch outcomes, not methods.

The core filter includes the three network classes; kinetic bases except stationary block breaking; transmission, gearbox, relays, controller, motor, crank, chain drives, gauges, steam and waterwheel sources; and belt root classes plus the belt connector item. Belt item transport/processing and unrelated machines are excluded. Windmill generation requires contraption assembly and is outside this report's kinetic package denominator.

**1552 missed core harness-gap branches in 774 groups remain.**

| Core area | Groups | Missed branches |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| RotationPropagator | 25 | 30 |
| TorquePropagator | 1 | 1 |
| base | 84 | 154 |
| belt | 331 | 654 |
| chainDrive | 20 | 36 |
| crank | 17 | 26 |
| gauge | 17 | 33 |
| gearbox | 8 | 18 |
| motor | 3 | 7 |
| simpleRelays | 114 | 268 |
| speedController | 13 | 20 |
| steamEngine | 47 | 121 |
| transmission | 31 | 46 |
| waterwheel | 63 | 138 |

The remaining groups below retain their source-reviewed reason-map explanations. These include reachable placement, interaction, network lifecycle and configuration paths that the current generators still omit. Environmental source, chunk lifecycle and mirror adapters identified in the reason map are separately classified and excluded from these harness-gap totals.

## Every remaining group

The source excerpt is the reported line and its next two lines from Create `b62e837`, for locating the branch. It is not a claim that every missed edge at that line has the same reachability.
